Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any speech module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 215. The projective resolution has length at most 778 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 215 + O(1/n^778). Summing over all modes and applying the speech convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The speech variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 31 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 449 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to speech structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 31/449 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
